========================================================
  BSHH Attack S2 — Malicious RSU                       
========================================================

  t=5.000   ①  V0 <-> V1: Normal V2V heartbeat exchange
  t=5.000   ②  V0/V1 -> Malicious RSU: DSRC heartbeat forwarding
  t=5.010   ③  RSU -> Controller: legitimate aggregate (CSMA)
  t=5.100   ④  Malicious RSU stores V0 heartbeat for replay
  t=10.000  ⑤  RSU replays stored HB impersonating victim
  t=10.000  ⑥  Controller holds conflicting liveness -> faulty routing

  LOG FORMAT: RSUs printed as RSU<idx>(ns3=<id>), vehicles as V<idx>(ns3=<id>)
  Every selected malicious RSU executes STEP ④, ⑤, ⑥.

========================================
  PAIR: ATTACKER=RSU0(ns3=205)  VICTIM=V172(ns3=177)  OTHER=V71(ns3=76)
========================================
[t=12.000]  STEP ①  NORMAL V2V HEARTBEAT EXCHANGE
  V172(ns3=177) -> V71(ns3=76) : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=12.000)
  V71(ns3=76) -> V172(ns3=177) : Heartbeat(Sender=V71(ns3=76), beacon_sending_time=12.000)

[t=12.000]  STEP ②  VEHICLES FORWARD HBs TO MALICIOUS RSU (DSRC broadcast)
  V172(ns3=177) --[DSRC broadcast]--> RSU0(ns3=205) : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=12.000)
  V71(ns3=76) --[DSRC broadcast]--> RSU0(ns3=205) : Heartbeat(Sender=V71(ns3=76), beacon_sending_time=12.000)

[t=12.010]  STEP ③  RSU FORWARDS LEGITIMATE AGGREGATE TO CONTROLLER (CSMA)
  RSU0(ns3=205) --CSMA--> Controller : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=12.000)  ACCEPTED
  RSU0(ns3=205) --CSMA--> Controller : Heartbeat(Sender=V71(ns3=76), beacon_sending_time=12.000)  ACCEPTED

[t=12.100]  STEP ④b  MALICIOUS RSU STORES A SECOND, EVEN-OLDER HB
  Attacker RSU    : RSU0(ns3=205)
  Victim          : V172(ns3=177)
  captured_packet : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=12.000)  (from an EARLIER legitimate exchange)
  Action          : RSU0(ns3=205) keeps this even-older copy for a SECOND replay duplicate (STEP ⑤)

========================================
  PAIR: ATTACKER=RSU0(ns3=205)  VICTIM=V172(ns3=177)  OTHER=V71(ns3=76)
========================================
[t=15.000]  STEP ①  NORMAL V2V HEARTBEAT EXCHANGE
  V172(ns3=177) -> V71(ns3=76) : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=15.000)

[t=15.000]  STEP ②  VEHICLES FORWARD HBs TO MALICIOUS RSU (DSRC broadcast)
  V172(ns3=177) --[DSRC broadcast]--> RSU0(ns3=205) : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=15.000)
  V71(ns3=76) --[DSRC broadcast]--> RSU0(ns3=205) : Heartbeat(Sender=V71(ns3=76), beacon_sending_time=15.000)

[t=15.010]  STEP ③  RSU FORWARDS LEGITIMATE AGGREGATE TO CONTROLLER (CSMA)
  RSU0(ns3=205) --CSMA--> Controller : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=15.000)  ACCEPTED
  RSU0(ns3=205) --CSMA--> Controller : Heartbeat(Sender=V71(ns3=76), beacon_sending_time=15.000)  ACCEPTED

[t=15.100]  STEP ④  MALICIOUS RSU STORES OLD HB
  Attacker RSU    : RSU0(ns3=205)
  Victim          : V172(ns3=177)
  captured_packet : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=15.000)  (from STEP ② legitimate exchange)
  Action          : RSU0(ns3=205) stores V172(ns3=177)'s heartbeat for replay (STEP ⑤)

[t=16.900]  STEP ④c  RSU SUPPRESSES VICTIM'S GENUINE CURRENT HB
  V172(ns3=177) --[DSRC broadcast]--> RSU0(ns3=205) : Heartbeat(Sender=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=16.900)  GENUINE, CURRENT
  Action          : RSU0(ns3=205) DOES NOT forward this genuine heartbeat to the controller (suppressed)
  Consequence     : controller's only liveness view for V172(ns3=177) at t~16.900 will come from the forged stale duplicates below

[t=17.000]  STEP ⑤  RSU REPLAY ATTACK
  Attacker RSU              : RSU0(ns3=205)
  Victim/claimed_sender     : V172(ns3=177)
  Packet source             : stored STEP ④ heartbeat captured from V172(ns3=177)
  RSU0(ns3=205) -> Controller : Heartbeat(claimed=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=15.000)
  physical_sender=RSU0(ns3=205)  claimed_sender=V172(ns3=177)
  Stale timestamp 15.000 < current t=17.000 -> replay evidence
  Different physical sender (RSU not vehicle) -> impersonation evidence

[t=17.000]  STEP ⑥  FAULTY ROUTING CONSEQUENCES
  Controller now holds conflicting liveness for V172(ns3=177):
    Latest entry : physical_sender=RSU0(ns3=205)  claimed_sender=V172(ns3=177)  beacon_sending_time=15.000  [FORGED]
  Packets may be routed using stale/non-existent liveness
  Expected impact: packet loss, added delay, degraded PDR
  <- ATTACK SUCCESS

  Sophistication: BASIC [no keys held] — RSU signs with own key, claims victim identity -> Step 1 MAC genuinely fails -> DROPPED at Stage-0 (Eq. 3.15)
[t=17.000000]  STEP ⑤b  RSU REPLAYS SECOND, EVEN-OLDER DUPLICATE
  RSU0(ns3=205) -> Controller : Heartbeat(claimed=V172(ns3=177), beacon_sending_time=12.000000)
  Controller now sees TWO conflicting stale entries for V172(ns3=177) under one identity
